QUESTIONS AND DESIGN
Does more auxiliary fitting improve tracking, the proposed correction, actual endpoint movement, and final quality?
Combined FD+KL only: compare 10 versus 1 auxiliary updates per generator step at the same calibrated fixed KL weight. Generator steps and LR are fixed; training compute is intentionally unequal.
All arms share Base, frozen evaluation encoder, real statistics, generator random seeds, and initial auxiliary weights/optimizer.
Different auxiliary counts consume equal prefixes of a separate deterministic stream; auxiliary LR is shared across conditions.
Both conditions optimize static FD + calibrated fixed-weight endpoint KL surrogate.
Calibrated weight gives initial RMS parameter-gradient ratio 0.5, then stays fixed; it is not dynamic gradient balancing.
Frozen-generator refits operate on copies. Independent held-out samples, identical probe noise, and current AdamW state are used.
A2 probes use endpoint-gradient alignment with CURRENT generator coarse-minus-fine error.
Local A3 probes use one-step endpoint movement versus that FROZEN pre-step error; they are not full post-training cancellation_cosine.
Full compensation uses the original fine-reference decomposition in the quality table.

INTERPRETATION LIMITS
DMD2-style tracking controls, not a full DMD2 implementation. KL remains endpoint-only Base-teacher pseudo-Huber.
Denoising MSE has irreducible conditional variance. Held-out reduction diagnoses fit improvement, not exact score accuracy.
The real teacher is held fixed. This sweep cannot rule out teacher bias; no high-dimensional oracle is introduced.
Independent-batch gradient scatter mixes minibatch and re-noising/reference noise. It is not a population gradient error estimate.
Trace reversals are descriptive and can occur during useful optimization. They are not proof of an unstable dynamical system.
Shadow probes evaluate few samples; final quality uses the original larger Corrected_Sinkhorn_divergence/FD evaluation. Neither is the true population KL.
Gradient ratios are before AdamW; actual parameter and endpoint alignment are measured separately.
Training time includes shared auxiliary warm-up per applicable arm. Diagnostic time is separate; preparation is shared.
run_seeds control post-training streams. They do not independently retrain Base or encoder.
Original initial A2/A3 probes use the combined objective at Base; feedback audits additionally isolate the auxiliary term at later checkpoints.
Final model archives are disabled. Internal initialization files are generated by this run; no automatic resume is implemented.
Smoke results validate execution only. Do not use smoke metrics to conclude generation quality improved.

SOURCE
https://arxiv.org/html/2405.14867v1 (Section 4.2 and Appendix C)

QUALITY METRIC DEFINITION
Corrected_W2_squared_Rd and Corrected_Sinkhorn_divergence_Rd: c=1, signed 0.5*(h(P1,Q1)+h(P2,Q2)-h(P1,P2)-h(Q1,Q2)); P1/P2 and Q1/Q2 are independent full clouds. No square root or clipping.
Corrected empirical W2 squared uses exact CPU uniform assignments, 5000 samples per cloud in production.
Corrected Sinkhorn uses full regularized OT_epsilon costs (debias=False); same-cloud self terms cancel from the four-term correction. epsilon=0.1, scaling=0.9, raw squared Euclidean cost; 50000 samples per cloud in production.
c=1 leaves samples unscaled. Replica seed offset=1000003; seeds/subsets are matched across Base and post-training. Target-target scalar baselines are cached per target and evaluation contract; no target tensor banks are retained.
Small shadow probes use corrected Sinkhorn and skip all empirical W2 work; smoke sample counts are explicit in shared settings. These are not full-quality estimates.
Online/KeOps uses linear storage, not linear pairwise compute; Sinkhorn is a numerical approximation, not an exact OT certificate. Corrected scores are finite-sample diagnostics, not guaranteed nonnegative population distances.
FD_phi_ratio_post_over_base = FD_phi(post,T)/FD_phi(Base,T), matched by branch and evaluation seeds. FD_phi_relative_gain=1-ratio. This cancels uniform feature scaling, not arbitrary changes of representation.
Raw FD values remain in diagnostic JSON and labeled diagnostic tables; the training loss retains its original normalization, spectral floor, and clamp.
training_surrogate is the optimized scalar, not measured population KL. Positive corrected transport gains mean Base minus post-trained.

GRADIENT FLOW AUDIT (before clipping)
Architecture: pre-normalized residual blocks, depth-scaled skips, LeakyReLU slope0.1, nonzero initialized output heads.
Monitoring is evidence, not a guarantee against vanishing gradients or score/feature underfitting.
Frozen teachers/encoders intentionally have no parameter updates; endpoint input gradients remain audited by A2/A3.
Small gradients can reflect convergence or exact zero score difference; flags alone do not establish vanishing gradients.
Detailed per-parameter pre-clip gradients: Gradient-health.jsonl.
